# Prototype inventory: functions and commands

Generated by tools/inventory (`just inventory --write`) from the prototype and reference-map.yaml. Do not edit.

Every public function, class and method, and every command line, of the prototype's scripts at `412b5d9`, read statically with `ast` (nothing is imported or run). Names that start with an underscore are private and left out. The summary is the first line of the docstring. Each file carries the home, phase and status of its entry in `reference-map.yaml`, and its functions inherit them: the map has no entries finer than a file. `prototype-inventory.json` holds the same data with more detail (decorators, line numbers, every option's keywords).

A command is a parser or click command with no subcommand under it. An option whose flag is computed rather than written out is shown as its source text. A `*` after an option marks it required.

## cad-analysis

Commands: 2 command lines. A script with no command line found is listed with its `__main__` block, if it has one.

| Script | Command | Summary | Options |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/ccx_failure.py` | – | Classify a CalculiX run that cannot give a valid result into an actionable no-result error: a failed ccx | – |
| `scripts/fea_conditions.py` | – | Material conditions and evidence of a static FEA case: where E, nu, density and yield come from, and | – |
| `scripts/fea_linearise.py` | – | Stress linearisation along a stress classification line (SCL), after ASME BPVC VIII-2 Annex 5-A. | – |
| `scripts/fea_modes.py` | – | What a static FEA case reports in each mode. | – |
| `scripts/fea_near_support.py` | – | Strength near a support or a load: the stress classification lines of #209 and the cross-check that gates them. | – |
| `scripts/fea_part_selection.py` | – | FreeCAD-independent STEP solid and occurrence selection for static analysis. | – |
| `scripts/fea_reaction.py` | – | A load case that names the stop reaction it comes from (#153). | – |
| `scripts/fea_static.py` | (script) | Run a static linear FreeCAD FEM analysis from a case YAML file. | `<case>`<br>`--hw`<br>`--version`<br>`--case-id`<br>`--replace`<br>`--out` |
| `scripts/fea_stress.py` | – | Two-mesh stress study: report a peak von Mises stress only when it has settled between two meshes. | – |
| `scripts/fea_stress_study.py` | – | The stress study's wiring: two solves' results in, the study's result fields out. | – |
| `scripts/freecad_lookup.py` | – | Shared lookup for the FreeCAD command-line executable. | – |
| `scripts/mesh_quality.py` | – | FreeCAD-independent validity and quality of a quadratic tetrahedral (C3D10) mesh. | – |
| `scripts/stop_extrema.py` | – | Extrema over the edges of an OCP shape. They bound a linear function (the arm of a contact patch) and the distance | – |
| `scripts/stop_reaction.py` | (script) | Stop reaction derived from the modelled contact | `--replace`<br>`--out` |
| `scripts/stop_statics.py` | – | Rigid statics of a hard stop, without a geometry kernel. | – |

## cad-drawings

Commands: 5 command lines. A script with no command line found is listed with its `__main__` block, if it has one.

| Script | Command | Summary | Options |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/assembly_check.py` | (script) | Re-check an assembly drawing package: balloons, parts list, leaders. | `<package>` |
| `scripts/assembly_drawing.py` | (script) | Generate the checked assembly drawing package of a built cad-model version. | `<hw>`<br>`--version*`<br>`--output-dir`<br>`--replace`<br>`--timeout`<br>`--jobs` |
| `scripts/assembly_geometry.py` | – | Assembly occurrences for assembly drawings, read once from a built assembly STEP. | – |
| `scripts/assembly_sheet.py` | – | Assembly sheets: view layout, item balloons, the parts list, assembly notes and page composition. | – |
| `scripts/assembly_techdraw_job.py` | – | FreeCAD-side TechDraw job for assembly_drawing.py; freecadcmd runs this file headless. | – |
| `scripts/completeness.py` | – | The part drawing completeness gate (drawing architecture §8): coverage rows and failure findings. | – |
| `scripts/drawing.py` | (script) | Generate checked, producer-ready part drawings from a built cad-model version. | `<hw>`<br>`--version`<br>`--part*`<br>`--output-dir`<br>`--replace`<br>`--timeout`<br>`--jobs` |
| `scripts/drawing_contract.py` | – | Drawing inputs as one verified context: the design record and one built model version, bound by hashes. | – |
| `scripts/drawing_document.py` | – | Drawing documents: sheet specifications, page composition and checked package publication. | – |
| `scripts/drawing_draft.py` | (script) | Propose drawing intent from a part's B-rep: a reviewable draft, never the record. | `<hw>`<br>`--version`<br>`--part*`<br>`--output-dir*`<br>`--timeout` |
| `scripts/drawing_intent.py` | – | Part drawing intent: the record's datums, features and requests, measured against the bound STEP. | – |
| `scripts/drawing_merge.py` | (script) | Merge a reviewed drawing draft's \`\`drawing\`\` block into the part's part.yaml. | `<hw>`<br>`--version`<br>`--part*`<br>`--draft*` |
| `scripts/export_job.py` | – | FreeCAD-side sheet export for sheet_export.py; freecadcmd runs this file headless. | – |
| `scripts/feature_coverage.py` | – | Whether a part sheet states every recognised manufacturing feature: located and sized, never overall only. | – |
| `scripts/feature_geometry.py` | – | Semantic features bound to a part's final STEP, and the geometry a drawing measures from them. | – |
| `scripts/feature_recognition.py` | – | Manufacturing features recognised on a part's final B-rep: what a complete drawing must dimension. | – |
| `scripts/freecad_runtime.py` | – | Locate headless FreeCAD and run the FreeCAD-side job scripts of the drawing pipeline. | – |
| `scripts/freecad_views.py` | – | View geometry across the FreeCAD boundary: TechDraw views as plain data for sheet composition. | – |
| `scripts/layout_check.py` | – | Pure layout checks over a generated drawing SVG. | – |
| `scripts/part_views.py` | – | View planning for part drawings: directions, projection arrangement, sections and details. | – |
| `scripts/section_hatch.py` | – | Section view fills: one lining (or a solid fill) per part in each section view, and the \`\`section_hatch\`\` gate. | – |
| `scripts/shared_module.py` | – | Load a sibling skill's script once under a shared name, safe for the drawing worker threads. | – |
| `scripts/sheet.py` | – | Part sheet composition: dimensions, callouts, datums and section marks on projected part views. | – |
| `scripts/sheet_export.py` | – | Export a checked drawing SVG to a sheet-sized PDF and PNG preview. | – |
| `scripts/techdraw_job.py` | – | FreeCAD-side job for drawing.py; freecadcmd runs this file headless. | – |

## cad-model

Commands: 5 command lines. A script with no command line found is listed with its `__main__` block, if it has one.

| Script | Command | Summary | Options |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/cad_model.py` | `run` | build hw/versions/\<label\>/ from the part model scripts | `<hw>`<br>`--label*`<br>`--kernel`<br>`--attach`<br>`--json` |
| `scripts/cad_model.py` | `check` | geometry checks on a built version | `<label>`<br>`--hw`<br>`--pair`<br>`--changed-only`<br>`--since-version`<br>`--exact`<br>`--allow-interference`<br>`--expect-solids`<br>`--out`<br>`--jobs`<br>`--cache`<br>`--no-cache`<br>`--json` |
| `scripts/cad_model.py` | `diff` | compare two built versions | `<a>`<br>`<b>`<br>`--hw`<br>`--out`<br>`--json` |
| `scripts/cad_model.py` | `latest` | point hw/versions/latest.json at the newest sealed version | `<hw>`<br>`--label*`<br>`--json` |
| `scripts/cad_model.py` | `store` | maintain the store hw/.cad/cache | `<action>`<br>`<hw>`<br>`--dry-run`<br>`--json` |
| `scripts/cadmodel/__init__.py` | – | cad-model: run part model scripts, then check and diff the versions they produce. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/agreement.py` | – | GLB \<-\> STEP agreement: whether each occurrence's mesh in assembly.glb depicts the STEP shape placed where | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/build.py` | – | \`run\`: build every part model into hw/versions/\<label\>/ and point hw/versions/latest.json at it. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/check.py` | – | \`check\`: blocking and advisory geometry checks over one built version. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/components.py` | – | The manufacturing components of a custom-made part: every disconnected solid of its manufacturing | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/diff.py` | – | \`diff\`: per-part volume and bounding-box deltas and exact shared / A-only / B-only volumes, and the | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/fillets.py` | – | Opt-in per-edge fillet fallback for authored CAD model scripts. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/fonts.py` | – | Resolve the TechDraw lettering font used for deterministic model text. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/holes.py` | – | Cylindrical bores of a part, measured on its read-back STEP solid so every kernel lists the same holes. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/manufacture.py` | – | The manufacturing outputs of a version's custom-made parts, and the checks that judge them. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/mesh.py` | – | Binary glTF from the assembly STEP, the occurrence -\> mesh-node map, mesh watertightness, and the binary | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/motion.py` | – | The cad-motion kinematics, pair policy, pair measurer, mesh bounds and incremental selection, loaded from its sibling skill; cad-model never re-implements posing or measuring. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/occ.py` | – | OCP measurements and Booleans shared by the kernel runners, safe edits, check, and diff. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/occ_derived.py` | – | Kernel-neutral Booleans adapted from AgentCAD 0.7.0. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/orientation.py` | – | How a manufactured part sits on the machine bed: the one owner of build orientation, for run and check. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/provenance.py` | – | What produced a cached output besides its inputs: the interpreter and installed distributions of the | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/record.py` | – | The cad-record modules, loaded from their sibling skill, and the record snapshot a version is built from. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/runner.py` | – | Kernel runner core: execute part model scripts in one kernel's environment, write per-part STEP. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/safe.py` | – | Safe Boolean edits for part model scripts, in either kernel. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/scriptenv.py` | – | Launch a PEP 723 script in the uv environment shared by every script with its dependency set. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/stepfile.py` | – | What a STEP file declares in its text: its schema and its length unit; and the read-back drift rule. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/store.py` | – | cad-model's part store: the cad-record store (\`cad-record/scripts/store.py\`), keyed by \`layout.input_key\`. | – |
| `scripts/cadmodel/threemf.py` | – | A deterministic 3MF writer and a strict reader, stdlib only. | – |
| `scripts/run_build123d.py` | – | build123d 0.13 kernel runner for \`cad_model.py run\`; protocol in cadmodel/runner.py. | `sys.exit(main('build123d', ('build123d', 'cadquery-ocp-novtk', 'bd_warehouse')))` |
| `scripts/run_cadquery.py` | – | CadQuery 2.8 kernel runner for \`cad_model.py run\`; protocol in cadmodel/runner.py. | `sys.exit(main('cadquery', ('cadquery', 'cadquery-ocp', 'vtk')))` |

## cad-motion

Commands: 9 command lines. A script with no command line found is listed with its `__main__` block, if it has one.

| Script | Command | Summary | Options |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/access.py` | (script) | Assembly order and geometric access of the recorded operations | `--step-mm`<br>`--min-step-mm`<br>`--replace`<br>`--out` |
| `scripts/code_shas.py` | – | The sha256 of a producer script and of every skill module it loads, for the recipe of what it publishes. | – |
| `scripts/continuous.py` | – | The between-sample check of cad-motion \`sweep.py\`: whether a pair can collide, or come closer | – |
| `scripts/coupling.py` | – | Coupled joint coordinates: typed laws, periodic profiles, slider-crank mechanisms and their DAG. | – |
| `scripts/engagement.py` | – | Where a path may touch a fitted partner by design: the engagement of an interference-type fit. | – |
| `scripts/freecad_assembly.py` | (script) | FreeCAD Assembly simulation with limit checks or a coupling cross-check | `--report-part`<br>`--time-step`<br>`--freecad`<br>`--job-dir`<br>`--case`<br>`--replace`<br>`--timeout`<br>`--frames`<br>`--start`<br>`--smoke` |
| `scripts/incremental.py` | – | Incremental selection for cad-model \`check\` and cad-motion \`sweep\`: what changed between two | – |
| `scripts/merge_sweeps.py` | (script) | Merge subsystem sweeps into the whole sweep's result | `--hw*`<br>`--version*`<br>`--case*`<br>`--inputs*`<br>`--replace` |
| `scripts/mesh_bound.py` | – | Conservative bounds on pose.py's pair distance from tessellated meshes, for pair_measure.py's | – |
| `scripts/motion.py` | `plan` | – | `--hw*`<br>`--json`<br>`--version*`<br>`--case`<br>`--plan`<br>`--print-default`<br>`--frames-out` |
| `scripts/motion.py` | `render` | – | `--hw*`<br>`--json`<br>`--version*`<br>`--case`<br>`--plan`<br>`--outputs`<br>`--size`<br>`--crosscheck`<br>`--replace`<br>`--out-dir` |
| `scripts/motion_glb.py` | – | Animated glTF from a cad-model version's assembly.glb; standard library only. | – |
| `scripts/pair_measure.py` | – | Pair measurements for cad-motion \`sweep.py\` and cad-model \`check\`: the one evaluator of both. | – |
| `scripts/pair_policy.py` | – | Declared pair relations between occurrences: fits, heat-set inserts and clearance requirements. | – |
| `scripts/plan.py` | – | Motion plans: choreography as data over cad-motion's Chain, with its coverage check; kernel-free. | – |
| `scripts/play.py` | (script) | Mechanism function under declared play and loads | `--steps`<br>`--replace`<br>`--out` |
| `scripts/pose.py` | (script) | Pose an assembly and write the posed STEP AP242 | `--hw*`<br>`--version`<br>`--step`<br>`--parts-dir`<br>`--attach`<br>`--set`<br>`--case`<br>`--replace`<br>`--out` |

| `scripts/subsystems.py` | – | Sweep subsystems: the record's subassemblies as owners of a sweep's pairs, and each one's period. | – |
| `scripts/sweep.py` | (script) | Kinematic sweep with interference and clearance checks | `--case`<br>`--replace`<br>`--worst-criterion`<br>`--steps`<br>`--grid`<br>`--end-effector`<br>`--include-pair`<br>`--min-clearance`<br>`--pair-clearance`<br>`--volume-tol`<br>`--max-poses`<br>`--time-limit`<br>`--jobs`<br>`--cache`<br>`--no-cache`<br>`--exact`<br>`--mesh-band`<br>`--spot-check`<br>`--no-spot-check`<br>`--subsystem`<br>`--no-continuous`<br>`--require-continuous`<br>`--continuous-grid-deg`<br>`--continuous-grid-mm`<br>`--continuous-safety`<br>`--continuous-depth`<br>`--smoke`<br>`--changed-only`<br>`--since-version`<br>`--worst-step` |

## cad-render

Commands: 2 command lines. A script with no command line found is listed with its `__main__` block, if it has one.

| Script | Command | Summary | Options |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/compare_renders.py` | (script) | Compare two cad-render bundles or scratch directories by decisions and PNG pixel parity. | `<left>`<br>`<right>` |
| `scripts/materials.py` | – | Part appearance from assets/materials.json: part.yaml \`material\` and \`finish\` -\> one PBR rule. | – |
| `scripts/parity_policy.py` | – | Decision and image parity policy shared by render caching and comparison. | – |
| `scripts/render.py` | (script) | Render verified cad-model versions to product PNG bundles via the static three.js PBR page. | `<hw>`<br>`--version`<br>`--views`<br>`--size`<br>`--subject`<br>`--jobs`<br>`--out-dir`<br>`--replace`<br>`--cache-dir`<br>`--json`<br>`--motion` |
| `scripts/versions.py` | – | Normalize built-version occurrences for render, viewer and model check. | – |

| File | Name | Signature | Summary | Home | Phase | Status |

## cad-viewer

Commands: 11 command lines. A script with no command line found is listed with its `__main__` block, if it has one.

| Script | Command | Summary | Options |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/cadviewer/__init__.py` | – | cad-viewer: drive OCP CAD Viewer and its side panel for a cad-model hardware record. | – |
| `scripts/cadviewer/capture.py` | – | The presenter: one headless Chromium page that keeps the panel open, and the captures it takes. | – |
| `scripts/cadviewer/gallery.py` | – | The preview gallery: what one hardware record holds to look at, per cad-model version. | – |
| `scripts/cadviewer/host.py` | – | The viewer host: OCP CAD Viewer processes, the side panel, and one HTTP API. | – |
| `scripts/cadviewer/motion.py` | – | Joint animation and section planes from cad-motion's kinematic chain; kernel-free. | – |
| `scripts/cadviewer/record.py` | – | Read one cad-model version of a hardware record into the viewer's scene and panel model. | – |
| `scripts/cadviewer/session.py` | – | The viewer session: one host process per harness session, found through a session file. | – |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `serve` | start or reuse the viewer host; prints its URL, PID and stop command | `--session`<br>`--panel-port`<br>`--port-a`<br>`--port-b`<br>`--width`<br>`--height`<br>`--idle-timeout` |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `stop` | stop the viewer host and its OCP viewers | `--session` |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `show` | push one version; the camera is kept on later pushes | `--session`<br>`<hw>`<br>`--version`<br>`--check` |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `compare` | push two versions for the A/B side-by-side with synced camera | `--session`<br>`<a>`<br>`<b>`<br>`--hw*`<br>`--check-a`<br>`--check-b` |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `gallery` | open HW in the preview gallery; prints its URL and every file path | `--session`<br>`<hw>`<br>`--version` |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `mode` | switch A/B: one version, or side-by-side | `--session`<br>`<mode>`<br>`--active` |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `parts` | hide, isolate, ghost the rest, or reset, as the panel buttons do | `--session`<br>`<action>`<br>`<target>` |
| `scripts/viewer.py` | `animate` | animate one joint within its assembly.yaml limits, or a motion plan case | `--session`<br>`--joint`<br>`--case`<br>`--from`<br>`--to`<br>`--steps`<br>`--seconds`<br>`--at` |

## agents and harness

Commands: 2 command lines. A script with no command line found is listed with its `__main__` block, if it has one.

| Script | Command | Summary | Options |
|---|---|---|---|
| `harness/generate.py` | (script) | Render neutral CAD agent sources as native harness agent profiles. | `--check` |
| `harness/install.py` | (script) | Install generated CAD agents and skills for supported coding harnesses. | `--harness*`<br>`--user`<br>`--project`<br>`--dry-run`<br>`--uninstall`<br>`--tier-model` |
